BEGIN experiment_memo.txt SHA256 19984e724c2453b83f78694c0c65333e629c2da0cfd976fee2bde84122a1fa71
GARDEN ELECTRICITY INTERFACE EXPERIMENT E1 — LOCAL PREDICTION FREEZE
Date: 2026-10-09. NONCANONICAL RESEARCH. Frozen before implementing/running E1.
This is a timestamp-free local protocol freeze, not independent preregistration.

Source read: GARDEN_SCIENCE_ALL_2026-10-09.txt (including 100 parked entries,
33 opportunities and 026 scripts); v15.10 abstraction delta; Tree Core v0.8.1;
v15.9.1 GCSC functional contracts and detailed semantic patch.
Scope: one electricity lifecycle interface, not a full Garden implementation.
It differs from 026: no hidden thermal compartment or structure selection.

PHYSICAL SITUATIONS
Converter current at transformer terminals -> period-averaged winding/stray
heat -> steady thermal balance -> permitted current -> equal-service choice.
AGENCY is the external experiment/controller only, never the transformer.

LAW AND DOMAIN
Let q_h=I_h/I_ref (dimensionless), M_p=sum_h q_h^2 h^p.
Loss L=P0 + D M0 + E M2 + S M0.8 [W]. This is the known harmonic transformer
loss approximation, not a fundamental invariant. Source: Senol, Bayram, Shi,
2025 accepted manuscript, equations 5, 7, 8; IEEE C57.110 source family.
https://strathprints.strath.ac.uk/92564/1/Senol-etal-IEEE-TECE-2025-Transformer-aging-under-harmonic-emissions-from-electric-vehicle.pdf
Constant coefficients, periodic currents, prescribed near-sinusoidal terminal
voltage, no DC, calibrated finite frequency band, linear magnetic operation,
balanced phases. Harmonic amplitudes refer to AGGREGATED terminal currents;
moments of separate sources must not simply be added before phasor summation.
No claim to represent saturation, protection, resonance, full hotspot fields,
ageing, voltage limits, or a complete transformer certification standard.
Thermal proxy: G(theta-Ta)=L [W], steady state only. It is not a certified
hotspot model. Useful power Puse=sqrt(3)*V_LL*I1 at unity displacement factor.
Electrical balance Psource=Puse+L+Paux. Paux is dissipated outside transformer.

FIXED SYNTHETIC PARAMETERS (not fitted, measured or manufacturer specifications)
I_ref=1000 A; V_LL=400 V; P0=1000 W; D=3000 W; E=300 W; S=150 W;
G=80 W/K; Ta=40 degC; theta_limit=105 degC; I_rms_limit=1200 A.
Spectra: fundamental I1 plus exactly 0.20 I1 at harmonic 5 or harmonic 13.
Both have THD=0.20 and I_rms=I1*sqrt(1.04).
At I1=950 A: predict low-harmonic thermal proxy <105 degC and high >105 degC.
Prediction: high-minus-low loss = (I1/Iref)^2 * .04 *
  [E*(13^2-5^2)+S*(13^.8-5^.8)] >1500 W at I1=950 A.
Maximum I1 is min(Irms_limit/sqrt(1.04),
 Iref*sqrt([G*(theta_limit-Ta)-P0]/K)),
where K=D*1.04+E*(1+.04*h^2)+S*(1+.04*h^.8).
Predict low-harmonic permissible I1 > high by >150 A.

COMPARATORS AND MAIN NULL
RMS-only assumes all spectrum has fundamental loss coefficient.
RMS+THD comparator assumes all nonfundamental power at h=5 (explicitly weak).
Conservative RMS+THD comparator assumes all harmonic power at h=13 (declared
finite band 5..13 only). Repaired interface sends M0,M2,M0.8 and coefficients.
Full-spectrum baseline sums each harmonic's loss term without moments.
Prediction: repaired interface equals full-spectrum baseline loss within
1e-9 W and every discrete equal-service decision on fixed synthetic cases.
This is a negative test of Garden-specific incremental physical/economic value.
No superiority versus modern full-state physical modelling is predicted.
For randomized verification: 200 spectra, fixed seed 20261009, orders
1,5,7,11,13; fundamental 500..1100 A and harmonic squared ratios total 0..0.09.
No learning, fitting, held-out mechanism, or blind-discovery claim.

ECONOMIC SCENARIO (conditional, not measured)
At I1=800 A both modes satisfy the SAME proxy thermal and current limits and
deliver identical useful power. Low-harmonic mode has assumed auxiliary loss
200 W, high-harmonic mode 0 W; assumed implementation cost 1200 currency units,
5-year straight-line annualization, zero discount, 50/year maintenance,
8000 operating hours/year and energy price 0.10 currency units/kWh.
Predict net energy saving >900 W after auxiliary loss; conditional annual net
benefit >400 currency units after annualized implementation and maintenance.
Full-spectrum baseline selects identical mode and achieves identical benefit;
incremental Garden gain versus that baseline is exactly zero before any extra
Garden implementation cost. These are hypothetical modes, not demonstrated
controllable waveforms or market cost quotes. No industry extrapolation.

FAILURE AND DOMAIN TESTS
At zero harmonic amplitude all comparators coincide. At E=S=0 RMS suffices.
At zero load loss is P0. Nonnegative coefficients imply nonnegative loss.
Numerical period integration of squared reconstructed current must agree with
Parseval RMS (orthogonal integer harmonics). Thermal/electrical balance residuals
must be <=1e-8 W. Optimizing a missing-spectrum model at 950 A should choose
zero-auxiliary high-harmonic mode and be flagged infeasible by full evaluation.

SECOND CYCLE — TEST REPRESENTATION VALIDITY
Construct two nonnegative harmonic energy distributions on 5,7,11,13 from the
null vector of rows [1,h^2,h^.8], with equal total harmonic energy .04 and same
fundamental. They share all retained moments. Add an explicitly ARTIFICIAL
residual coefficient r_h=epsilon*(h/13)^1.5 for h>1, epsilon=5000 W.
Predict moments still equal within 1e-10, but exact perturbed loss differs.
For any spectrum, |L_true-L_model|<=epsilon*M_harm. Hence absolute temperature
error <=epsilon*M_harm/G. A robust thermal gate must include that loss margin;
without a coefficient-error bound the compressed interface is UNKNOWN.
For a common action set made feasible by this robust margin, cost-only regret
is <=2*epsilon*max_a(M_harm(a))*hours*price/1000, excluding investment costs
already included equally. This is the standard bounded-model-error argument.
Deviation from h^2 is already studied, e.g. Liu et al. 2016:
https://ietresearch.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1049/iet-epa.2015.0559
The artificial h^1.5 term is a falsification instrument, not a physical claim.

PROMOTION BOUNDARIES
Any predicted numerical mismatch beyond tolerance falsifies implementation or
declared model contract. Real-transformer deployment needs independently measured
spectrum, coefficients, thermal fields and admissible controls with full safety
constraints. EMPIRICAL_VALIDATION=NOT_RUN; FRONTIER_NOVELTY=NOT_ESTABLISHED;
REAL_ECONOMIC_VALUE=UNKNOWN; BLIND_METHOD_ADVANTAGE=NOT_TESTED.

END experiment_memo.txt

4.10 ELECTRICITY END-USE: COMPRESSED AIR, A DOCUMENTED LARGE SAVING
Executed 2026-10-09. NONCANONICAL RESEARCH. Source base ce2b1eaf6f37c31b845202655b09b8694791dd81.

Question: find one physical situation, apply the v15.10/TREE mapping and identify substantial cost savings. Selected boundary: electricity -> industrial compressed air -> useful production, parasitic discharge and recurrent maintenance. This is a retrospective worked example of known engineering, not a blind discovery. The historical intervention was performed by the plant team, not Garden. This bounded branch does not replace the electricity lifecycle map or declare it complete.

4.10.1 Historical economic anchor and limits
DOE's January 2001 Visteon Monroe case reports over $560,000/year in savings, an 11.5% electricity-cost reduction and more than 50% lower compressed-air consumption per production unit. Reduced demand enabled three reciprocating compressors to be taken offline. The reported improvement occurred during the earlier plant program; these are historical dollars, not a 2026 quote. Project capital cost and raw interval readings are unavailable in the inspected case. Thus full net present value cannot be reconstructed. Do not attribute the outcome to Garden or to a single isolated repair.

Source owner: U.S. DOE, DOE/ORNL-019, January 2001, hosted by Compressed Air Challenge; pages 1-3, Summary and Results.
https://www.compressedairchallenge.org/data/sites/1/media/library/casestudies/Visteon-CompAir-Steel.pdf
Exact PDF bytes: 322561; SHA256 77b7809a6ad8f8220017535cb549005c1b200a9e7fd9a9b7018e67abb7ece2e6.
Source inventory caveat: the initial stated total horsepower and later reported base/peak/offline horsepower do not reconcile by simple addition. Equipment/time scope needs clarification; no energy result here is derived from those horsepower totals.

Separate cost/payback cross-check within the same technology: DOE's November 2003 American Water Heater case reports 2,345,000 kWh/year and $129,000/year energy savings, plus $31,000/year maintenance/cooling savings; $228,000 investment gives 17.1-month simple payback. This was a combined retrofit, including differentiated treatment, pressure, storage, controls, leaks and cooling. Its site-specific benefits cannot be assigned to an arbitrary factory.
Source owner: U.S. DOE, DOE/GO-102003-1716, pages 1-4.
https://www1.eere.energy.gov/manufacturing/tech_assistance/pdfs/bp_cs_americanwaterheater.pdf
Exact PDF bytes: 834841; SHA256 b4e6067e923828bb75095ed997d0fadbe0f13b81b49ffc3f67d78b8083468bb4.

4.10.2 How the actual Garden design is used
Read V15_10_ABSTRACTION_GCSC_DELTA.md in full and TREE_CORE_v0.8.1_2026-09-23.txt sections 1.2-1.8, 2.4-2.6 and required per-record fields. Their exact hashes appear in the executable view below; source boundaries from section 4.9 remain in force.
ROOT: identities, source ownership, scope, status axes, ten facets and no-self-admission.
TRUNK: observations -> typed situations -> constrained candidates -> comparison -> operator authorization -> measurement and correction. No physical action is performed here.
BOUNDARY: compressor performance, pneumatic balances, terminal service and cost contracts remain specialist equations, not universal Garden laws.
Objects: TIME is cycle/shift; SPACE is plant/branch; THING is compressor, air, receiver or pipe; EVENT is pressure change, production stop or new leak; ACTION is repair or mode selection; AGENCY is the authorized human operator; RULE is the scoped physical/engineering obligation; VALUE is net avoided cost; CONTEXT is production/ambient/asset condition; CLAIM is a source-reported or calculated saving. No air molecule is assigned authority, ownership or agency.
Relations used in the bounded semantic records include frames, dependsOn, acts and references. Additional explicit process dependencies carry typed state/flow ports. The 24-relation vocabulary is not forced into every situation. Semantic plausibility is not physical causation or normative authority.
Seven Forms: CONSTRUCT equipment/network; CONTRACT service and typed interface; STATE pressure/temperature/inventory/mode; RELATION dependencies; PROCESS production/repair/control sequence; RULE balances and limits; PROJECTION summaries such as total airflow or aggregate bill.
All ten facet bindings are retained in compressed_air_case.py, including source/evidence, effect safety, resource termination, recovery and human authorization. Unknown real-plant inputs remain unresolved. Record-valued ports are intentionally opaque and need field/time/calibration qualification before operational use.
This reuses SIC-0.1's finite research audits; it does NOT claim to execute full GSL, SAL interpretation registries, TREE_CORE-r6 canonical S0/S1/S2, admission, or a source-retention gate. It does not repair earlier TREE_CORE integrity debt. A structural graph check cannot self-admit a financial or physical claim.

4.10.3 Cross-situation audit and three search operators
The economically material chain is: useful demand and terminal quality -> leakage/idle demand -> receiver inventory -> compressor operating mode -> metered electricity -> net cost. The key engineering risk is assuming that less wasted air automatically means proportionally less electrical consumption.

GAP-CA1 | Demand reduction -> compressor modes -> electrical power.
Projection-Loss: Y=(delivered flow, header pressure) omits running/unloaded/stopped modes. In the explicit toy model below, q=1 kg/s and the same service pressure are compatible with 200 or 260 kW depending on whether a second unit remains unloaded. Thus Y is not sufficient to predict power. Adding modes and valid compressor maps repairs this particular projection. KNOWN engineering, not a new invariant.

GAP-CA2 | Repair -> smaller supply requirement -> unit shutdown.
Necessity-Challenge: challenge 'both units must remain on'. With the synthetic useful demand 1 kg/s and leakage 0.2 kg/s, one 1 kg/s unit is initially insufficient. After the leak is eliminated, one meets the specified steady demand. This is admissible only when transient demand, response time, pressure/quality and reserve obligations also allow it. A 5% sustained excess demand defeats the one-unit policy; a small receiver cannot rescue it indefinitely.

GAP-CA3 | Feasible actions -> real money.
Decision-Sufficiency: retaining power/mode data changes the cost comparison. Under the fixed toy contract, stopping the redundant unloaded unit after repair saves an additional 60 kW, or $48,000/year gross at the assumed hours/tariff. It is preferred only if its extra annualized implementation, maintenance, interruption and reliability costs total less than that amount. Against a conventional optimizer with the same information and constraints, Garden's incremental operating benefit is zero in this test. Audit-labor or faster-deployment benefits remain unmeasured.

GAP-CA4 | Stored air -> short measurement window.
Equal production and equal endpoint pressure alone do not prove equal energy service. Track receiver mass and thermal state: m=P_abs*V/(R*T). Compare equal initial/final stored mass and energy, or include a separately justified storage correction. Otherwise draining the receiver counterfeits savings.

GAP-CA5 | Compressor-room readings -> production endpoint quality.
The output contract includes pressure, moisture/contamination and accepted product output. The first local review found that dew-point observation was absent from the sensor join; it was added, together with a product-quality record. Binding a port does not supply a missing sensor or verify quality.

GAP-CA6 | Repair -> recurrence and baseline drift.
Leaks can recur; production mix, operating hours, ambient conditions, compressor maps and tariffs can change. Validate persistence and compare matched production service. Off-shift airflow includes legitimate uses until inspection establishes otherwise.

Ranked action within this example: first verify idle/leak demand and supply-mode response; second coordinate repairs with a qualified dispatch policy; third retain monitoring against recurrence. All six interface checks concern established mechanisms or missing site evidence. No frontier-novel claim survives because none is made. Physical joint satisfaction of all connected dependencies remains UNKNOWN without plant data.

4.10.4 Quantitative equation contract and executed counterfactual
Author model CA-POWER-001 (synthetic; not fitted to either historical plant):
P(q,n)=n*P_u+(P_l-P_u)*q/q_max.
q is total delivered mass flow [kg/s]; q_max is each identical unit's capacity [kg/s]; n is an integer number running; P_l and P_u are loaded and unloaded electrical powers [kW]. Both terms have kW dimensions. Domain: 0<=q<=n*q_max; 0<=P_u<=P_l. This is a cycle-averaged load/unload model, with negligible transition losses, constant pressure, unchanged auxiliaries and equivalent terminal service. It is not a thermodynamic law or an instantaneous safe-dispatch model.
Inputs: q_max=1 kg/s, P_l=200 kW, P_u=60 kW; useful demand=1 kg/s; leak flow changes 0.2->0 kg/s. Hours=8000/year; tariff=$0.10/kWh. These are illustrative parameters, not extracted plant observations.
Baseline: q=1.2,n=2 ->288 kW.
Repair only, both units remain running: q=1,n=2 ->260 kW.
Repair plus feasible shutdown: q=1,n=1 ->200 kW.
Consequently repair alone saves28 kW; coordinated operation saves88 kW total. Additional dispatch benefit conditional on repair is60 kW. Do not add the28 kW again to the88 kW.
Gross annual saving=88 kW*8000 h/year*$0.10/kWh=$70,400/year. Net is this amount minus actual added annual expenses and annualized investment. One hundred hypothetical installations satisfying the same model would give $7.04 million/year gross before those costs. This arithmetic is NOT an identified fleet, an adoption forecast or evidence of 100 eligible plants.

Falsifiable prediction: for equipment independently shown to satisfy this fixed contract, matched operation should reduce average package power from288 to200 kW while preserving endpoint service and storage. A held-out power difference inconsistent with88 kW after measurement uncertainty rejects the model's applicability. Obtain uncertainty from meters/calibration and fitted-map residuals before freezing a field acceptance interval; none is invented here.
Nulls: if removed leakage is replaced by equal blow-off, power saving is0 in the model. If a second running unit is required for the operating envelope, its shutdown benefit is unavailable. If remaining demand is1.05 kg/s, one1 kg/s unit is infeasible. A1 m3 ideal-gas receiver at300 K with20 kPa usable pressure band contains only0.232288 kg usable mass and covers a0.05 kg/s shortfall for4.645761 seconds, assuming isothermal behavior. Finite storage cannot cover a permanent deficit.
The constraint-derived minimum-unit rule and conventional exhaustive feasible-action optimization agree at all40 tested nonzero demand values from0.05 to2 kg/s. This is a known-model equivalence check, not a blinded Garden method benchmark. No real hardware or MEASUR executable was run.

4.10.5 Prior art, independent attack and remaining qualification
DOE's compressed-air sourcebook explains that partial-load controls and unloaded consumption materially affect power, and that storage and compressor controls must be considered together. AIRMaster+ already accounts for interactions between efficiency measures. Their existence rejects novelty for the basic leak-repair/dispatch combination.
https://www1.eere.energy.gov/manufacturing/tech_assistance/pdfs/compressed_air_sourcebook.pdf
https://www.energy.gov/sites/prod/files/2014/04/f15/airmaster_user_manual.pdf (scenario and measure interactions, printed pp141-147)
Modern comparator: ORNL MEASUR documentation inspected on2026-10-09, displayed v1.2.5, describes cycle-average compressor power with blowdown, off-load, reload, pump-up, modulation and storage timing. This is more detailed than CA-POWER-001 and is an appropriate baseline for actual site work. No superiority over that model is claimed.
https://industrialresources.ornl.gov/measur/suite/docs/group__load__unload__compressor__assessment
https://industrialresources.ornl.gov/measur/suite/docs/group__compressed__air__assessments

A separate adversarial agent inspected the sources, ran the script and independently checked arithmetic. It identified four corrections: retain kinetic/potential terms in the exact energy balance; bind terminal moisture/product quality into measurement; call60 kW the conditional dispatch benefit rather than an identified factorial interaction; restrict missing project-cost status to Visteon. Corrections were applied. Same-family agent review is not independent external certification.
Executable checks also inject an incompatible mass-flow unit and remove the sequencer's mode-command edge; both become unresolved. All plant states still require domain/evidence qualification. Eight consumer inputs have no bound external site source, and twelve situation-specific unknowns remain. Coverage counts below are structural inventory counts, not a probability of savings or completeness of industrial science.

Economic conclusion: a documented historical opportunity of over half a million dollars per year exists in this electricity end-use category. The mapping identifies why the repair, demand, storage, operating-mode and verification steps must be considered together. It cannot promise the same result at an unspecified site, quantify a net Visteon investment return from missing costs, or establish an additional Garden saving over expert practice.

MASS [EXACT_CLASSICAL_BALANCE] dm/dt = sum(mdot_in)-sum(mdot_out) | kg/s on both sides; all leaks, drains, purge, blow-off and storage included

PRESSURE [ENGINEERING_CONSTRAINT] p_terminal(t) >= p_required(t), within upper rated limits | Pa; constraints at each consumer across transient events, not just mean header
QUALITY [ENGINEERING_CONSTRAINT] dewpoint_terminal <= specified_limit; contamination within specification | K; pressure-dew-point definition, operating pressure and product requirements bound
MODE_POWER [EMPIRICAL_MODEL] P=n*P_u+(P_l-P_u)*q/q_max; 0<=q<=n*q_max | kW; identical ideal load/unload units, cycling losses ignored; measured maps supersede
LEAK [SCOPED_CONSTITUTIVE] mdot_leak=C(T,gas,orifice)*P_abs | kg/s; choked ideal-gas branch at fixed temperature only, not universal leak law
DISPATCH [ENGINEERING_CONSTRAINT] available_supply+permitted_storage_draw >= demand+losses | kg/s; recovery, reserve, pressure, starts and failure response are separate limits
SERVICE [ENGINEERING_CONSTRAINT] delivered output, quality and reliability must meet the same contract | No dollar saving from reducing required service or omitting repair/quality costs
MEASUREMENT [EVIDENCE_CONSTRAINT] align clocks, calibrate meters, retain uncertainty and before/after context | UNKNOWN measurements cannot establish savings or cause physical actuation
VALUE [ECONOMIC_CONDITION] net=avoided_total_bill+avoided_maintenance-added_cost-annualized_capital | USD/year; equal service and inventory; no double counting energy and bill savings

4.11 INDUSTRIAL SITUATION-TO-INVARIANT INVESTIGATION — 9 OCTOBER 2026
Status: NONCANONICAL RESEARCH. No scientific admission or production control.

RESULT FIRST
This pass did not establish the requested novel industrial process with large-scale savings. Six physically motivated candidate cycles were mapped and attacked across rinsing, drying and membrane shutdown/cleaning. Broad mechanisms were already known; two narrow interventions remain unverified and economically ungrounded. These are local negative findings, not evidence of domain exhaustion.
A separate, concrete result is a reproduced reference-state inconsistency in a current industrial energy calculator and a tested research correction. The calculation can predict a change in energy with no physical change. Later prior-report review found existing concerns about this calculator's measured/rated pressure logic, so this is a reproduced specific counterexample, NOT an established previously unknown defect. Fixing a displayed saving does not itself save electricity. Historical novelty, deployed incidence and incremental economic value remain UNKNOWN.

Materiality, not a target record count, justified expansion. Recursion is a fresh bounded source-seeded research cycle; S1 outputs were not automatically self-fed or self-admitted.

A new engineering configuration can use known physics; a conventional model reproducing it after being given the configuration does not by itself defeat design novelty. Here, broad novelty rejections depend on actual earlier mechanisms/configurations; unresolved narrow designs are not rejected merely because their equations are old.

4.11.2 COMPRESSOR REFERENCE-STATE CLOSURE: ACTUAL GAP AND REPAIR
Physical observation: a measured compressor power belongs to a particular operating pressure, atmosphere, useful flow, temperature, control state and efficiency. Transporting only a kW number through an intervention calculator can detach it from those conditions.
Generated comparison obligation: an unchanged physical operating state, hours and tariff must yield zero intervention saving. This is an identity invariant of comparison, not a new conservation law. It holds even if the detailed power model is inaccurate, provided both sides describe the same state.
The finite graph below refines the observation -> representation -> intervention -> value interfaces in the previously mapped compressed-air process. The physical boundary is compressor operation and useful air delivery; subsequent nodes are observation, computation or human decision situations, not additional physical processing stages.

P01 Intake and compress air; mass and energy ports.
P02 Deliver required air through network; duty identity, operating pressure, local atmosphere.
P03 Measure baseline power and its operating context.
P04 Select measured or nameplate reference and preserve its origin.
P05 Serialize that reference across UI/library boundary.
P06 Select a feasible proposed pressure at unchanged useful duty.
P07 Evaluate both physical states from the same reference.
P08 Annualize both states with matched hours and tariff.
P09 Compare avoided bill with all added annual costs.
P10 Authorized intervention and independent matched metering (NOT PERFORMED).
P11 Compare prediction with evidence before admitting any benefit (field evidence UNKNOWN).
Directed dependencies: P01->P02, P01->P03, P02->P03, P03->P04, P04->P05,
P02->P06, P05->P07, P06->P07, P07->P08, P08->P09, P09->P10,
P02->P10, P10->P11, P08->P11.
Chronological edges and contextual dependencies are explicitly distinguished from claims of experimental execution.

Garden bindings: THING compressor/network/meter; SPACE control volume and meter location; TIME operating and sampling intervals; EVENT compression/measurement/intervention; ACTION authorized selection/intervention; AGENCY operator only; RULE balance/reference/service obligations; VALUE net cost; CONTEXT physical duty and atmosphere; CLAIM prediction/evidence. Meaningful typed relations in the executable graph include CONTEXT frames EVENT, AGENCY acts ACTION and CLAIM references VALUE. Other physical/dependency relations are explicit graph edges. Not all24relations are assigned to every node. No agency, ownership or delegation is assigned to gas.
Forms: CONSTRUCT for physical equipment; STATE for operating quantities; PROCESS for chronology; RELATION for dependencies; RULE for obligations; CONTRACT for equation scope; PROJECTION for measured/serialized/annualized states. Ten cross-cutting facets are bound in code: identity/lifecycle, scope/context, epistemics/provenance, authority/human boundary, effects/safety, dependency/validity, resource/termination, privacy/retention, audit/explanation, recovery/evolution. A binding is not proof that missing site evidence has been supplied.

Invariant/constraint register (families, not new physical laws):
MASS: exact classical control-volume balance dm/dt=sum(mdot_in)-sum(mdot_out); kg/s; include leaks, purge and storage. Counterexample is an independently closed balance beyond uncertainty.
ENERGY: exact classical control-volume balance dU/dt=Qdot+Wdot+enthalpy_in-enthalpy_out; W; kinetic/potential terms if material. Missing ports invalidate application.
SERVICE: engineering requirement for identical useful flow, minimum terminal pressure, quality and availability; site limits UNKNOWN. Reduced service falsifies a comparable-saving claim.
ANCHOR: representation requirement that power retains basis, reference pressure, atmosphere and duty identity. A scalar kW value alone cannot select the correct physical state.
WORK: scoped constitutive approximation P=Pf+(Pref-Pf)F(p,a)/F(pref,aref), under unchanged flow, temperature, efficiency and mode. Mismatched field curves invalidate quantitative saving, not identity.
IDENTITY: derived comparison invariant, identical states/hours/tariff imply zero delta cost. Explicit no-change test is the oracle.
COMPOSITION: derived model invariant F(c)/F(a)=[F(b)/F(a)]*[F(c)/F(b)] for one unchanged model/duty; no repeated or mixed-reference correction.
VALUE: economic condition net=verified avoided bill-all added annual costs; currency/year; prediction errors are not electricity saved.
EVIDENCE: claims retain independent status axes; hashes establish source identity, not truth.

Executable coverage: 11 curated situations;14 explicit transitions;9 constraint families;24 applicability bindings;4 incoming-edge pairs enumerated. One selected representation handoff has three missing semantic requirements (reference atmosphere, power basis, duty identity). All14transitions receive structural port checks; four enumerated combinations are not four validated scientific mechanisms. The repaired research graph has0missing declared transfers/0unbound declared inputs. Its physical completeness, fullSAL coherence and real-data sufficiency remain UNKNOWN. External-required placeholders bind the obligation to obtain data, not actual data.
Human-authored ports and laws drive these diagnostics. This is not blind automated extraction of all defects from arbitrary source code. The invariant was generated from a mapped comparison and then tested against actual implementation; it was not assigned from a candidate number/title.

4.11.3 PINNED IMPLEMENTATION AND COUNTEREXAMPLES

https://github.com/ORNL-AMO/MEASUR-Tools-Suite/tree/0669b456c67bd8b9c1656a83cd7d35d68a2701d5
Source module: src/treasureHunt/compressed_air_pressure_reduction.cpp.
The public API/documents permit measured and rated power. Current baseline arithmetic uses entered power directly; modification applies a pressure-work ratio using local atmosphere in one state and standard14.7psia in the reference. Baseline pressure is unused. Power basis is not represented in the library input.
Official documentation: https://industrialresources.ornl.gov/measur/suite/docs/group__compressed__air__pressure__reduction__calculator

Desktop source trace commit: 562402815720434463c5bca62747bc4e0a10d0ed (8October2026).
https://github.com/ORNL-AMO/AMO-Tools-Desktop/tree/562402815720434463c5bca62747bc4e0a10d0ed
Dependency pin: measur-tools-suite@1.2.6-rc.350.1. The installed WASM/browser was NOT executed. Findings apply to inspected source and compiled current Suite module; a released-binary incidence claim needs separate reproduction.
The normal measured-mode form sets rated-pressure field equal to operating pressure; it forwards raw measured kW. Therefore an arbitrary125psig-rated/100psig-operating witness is valid at the public API but is NOT a faithful normal interactive measured-mode case. This distinction was caught by independent caller inspection and corrected before reporting.
The local-atmosphere counterexample survives that form transformation: measured100kW, operating100psig, proposed100psig, local atmosphere12psia. The copied rated-pressure field is100. Existing source predicts111.7955418159kW for the unchanged state. At8000h/y and an ASSUMED$0.10/kWh this is a fictitious$9436.43345/y cost increase. The correct identity result is exactly zero saving.
At the same local conditions but proposed90psig, source predicts105.5619816225kW and therefore$4449.58530/y extra cost. A consistently normalized, fully pressure-scalable model predicts94.4241424191kW and$4460.68606/y gross saving. These are synthetic model predictions, not a measured installation result.
Unaffected null: local atmosphere14.7psia, operating=proposed=rated100psig gives0saving in both implementations. The upstream fixture uses standard atmosphere and coincident pressure anchors, so that fixture alone cannot expose the mismatch.
API-only witness: at14.7psia, measured100kW at100psig but suppliedrated125psig, unchanged100psig yields$9214.95109/y fictitious saving. Lowering to90psig yields$13307.12241/y claimed saving versus$4624.89906/y in the normalized model. Do not generalize this nameplate mismatch to the normal measured-mode UI.

4.11.4 EQUATION CONTRACT AND REPAIR
Let p denote gauge discharge pressure, a absolute inlet atmospheric pressure, b=(k-1)/k with k=1.395, and F(p,a)=(1+p/a)^b-1.
At fixed mass flow, inlet temperature and efficiency, ideal compression work is proportional to F. Anchor power must bind Pref[kW], pref[psig], aref[psia], basis, physical duty identity and optionally pressure-independent Pf[kW]. Both pressures in p/a use the same pressure unit; adding p+a converts gauge to absolute in the same state.
For a common anchor:
  Pj = Pf + (Pref-Pf)*F(pj,aj)/F(pref,aref), j=0,1.
  annual_gross = h*(P0-P1)*tariff.
For measured baseline power at the local state, pref=p0 and aref=a0. Thus at unchanged state P1=P0=Pref, regardless of local atmosphere. For rated reference, compute BOTH baseline and proposed powers from the same rated anchor; never compare raw rated power with an adjusted operating power.
Derivation of identity: equal states give equal work factors. Derivation of composition: intermediate factor cancels. Derivation of anchor re-expression invariance: converting Pref to an equivalent second reference and using it for both states cancels the second reference factor. These are established algebra/thermodynamics, not newly discovered theorems.
Dimensions: F and its ratio dimensionless; P kW; h h/year; energy kWh/year; tariffUSD/kWh; resultUSD/year. p>=0, a>0, referencep>0, 0<=Pf<=Pref, finite values. The zero-compression reference is excluded because its work factor cannot normalize measured power. The p->0 endpoint is a formal model limit, not qualified compressor operation.
Applicability: noncentrifugal pressure-only screening model; same useful mass flow, inlet temperature, efficiency, load/control mode and fixed auxiliary term. Flow-dependent leaks, artificial demand, cycling, off-load power, dryer demand and receiver transients need separate preserved relationships. Unknown scalable fraction cannot be silently set to1 in a field claim. A duty-ID string is an explicit caller obligation, not proof the duty is equal.
Repair implemented in industrial_transition_audit.py: immutable PowerAnchor with explicit reference/basis/context, shared evaluation of both states, invalid/ambiguous inputs rejected, and a fixed-power term. This is a working research comparator, NOT a production patch to MEASUR. It does not modify third-party software or operate machinery.
Integration requirement: current Desktop modification callers overwrite the pressure field with proposed pressure; simply changing the library denominator to that field would break the caller contract. A production correction must preserve explicit immutable reference fields across both adapters and library, migrate stored cases, retain measured/rated intent, and verify the actual WASM build.
Strong comparator: ordinary consistently referenced thermodynamic compressor-work evaluation gives exactly the same corrected numbers. Garden has no numerical advantage once both receive the correct model. The contribution in this pass is the structured identification/test of a violated interface obligation.

4.11.5 THREE DISCOVERY OPERATORS APPLIED
Projection-Loss: dropping power basis/reference context maps physically different100kW measurements to the same scalar. Predicted response to a pressure intervention differs with their actual reference conditions. The missing information is reference ownership, not a proposed exotic hidden state. A complete anchor restores the distinction.
Necessity-Challenge: ask whether a measured local power must first be interpreted at standard atmosphere/nameplate pressure. The same-state counterexample disproves that as a necessary comparison step; retain the actual measured reference. This does not challenge minimum process pressure or any safety requirement.
Decision-Sufficiency: preserve reference context only insofar as it changes a feasible decision with positive incremental value after correction/qualification costs. The synthetic12psia/90psig example reverses the sign of estimated benefit, so it can change the investment choice. No new measurement is necessarily needed: the UI already collects local atmosphere. This is preservation of available information, not a claim that additional sensors always pay.

4.11.6 ECONOMIC ASSESSMENT: CONDITIONAL, NOT REALIZED
Assume a feasible90psig intervention,100kW measured baseline at100psig/12psia,8000hours, tariff$0.10/kWh and$2000/y all-in annualized intervention cost. These numbers define a test scenario, not a site forecast.
Original formula: estimatedgross=-$4449.59/y -> reject.
Corrected fully-scalable model: estimatedgross=$4460.69/y -> conditionalnet=$2460.69/y -> accept.
If only fraction f of baseline power is pressure-scalable, correctedgross=$4460.68606*f/y and conditionalnet=$4460.68606*f-$2000/y. Break-even requires f>0.4483615; at f=.70 net=$1122.48/y. Pressure feasibility, actual efficiency, leakage/control interactions and real project cost could reverse the result.
The displayed-error magnitude is not recoverable energy. Incremental repair value requires real decisions that differ, qualified net outcomes and adoption, less software verification/deployment costs. A suitable population estimand is N*eligible_fraction*adoption*E[net_outcome(repaired_choice)-net_outcome(existing_choice)|eligible]-annual_repair_cost. All population, incidence, adoption and measured-outcome inputs are UNKNOWN. Multiplying an assumed single-machine figure by an invented industrial fleet would not establish large-scale savings. No such total is claimed.

Material adverse evidence:

https://github.com/ORNL-AMO/AMO-Tools-Desktop/issues/4523 already distinguishes field-measured power/current operating pressure from nameplate input and discusses correcting the baseline.
https://github.com/ORNL-AMO/AMO-Tools-Desktop/issues/4527 specifies copying baseline operating pressure into the rated-pressure field for measured mode.
https://github.com/ORNL-AMO/AMO-Tools-Desktop/issues/7876 describes a different no-change/performance-point problem in the full assessment. It is not evidence that all no-op violations are this defect.
No exact12psia counterexample was found in the reviewed records, but the broader defect family was already acknowledged. Final status is REPRODUCED_SPECIFIC_COUNTEREXAMPLE; historical novelty UNRESOLVED, broad conceptual novelty NOT CLAIMED. The pressure-work repair is established mathematics. Do not market this as Garden-originated new thermodynamics or a previously unrecognized industry-wide opportunity.

For the remaining physical candidates: use the branch-specific bench protocols below. The rinse thermal-window idea needs density, geometry, mouth solute-export and throughput/drag-out measurements against optimized rotation/targeted rinsing. The drying reserve idea needs material retained energy and scheduling demand that overcome its current small energy bound. Neither warrants invented large-scale savings.
Status axes: hypothesis formulatedYES; reference-identity derivationVALID_WITHIN_CONTRACT; original-code counterexampleREPRODUCED; research correctionTESTED; scopedpriorartCHECKED_WITH_ADVERSE_RESULTS; historicalnoveltyUNRESOLVED; novelphysicalprocessNOT_ESTABLISHED; fieldvalidationNOT_PERFORMED; economicsCONDITIONAL_ONLY; measuredGardenincrementUNKNOWN; matched-budgetmethodsuperiorityNOT_TESTED.
No branch is declared scientifically exhausted. Known mechanisms are retained as negative controls and low-value expansions are stopped pending new physical evidence.


4.11.10 RANKED GAP REGISTER — RESEARCH PRIORITY, NOT PROVEN VALUE
1. C-PRESSURE: reference atmosphere/basis lost across measured-state -> calculator transition. Concrete original-code counterexample and research correction reproduced. Best verified output of this pass; related issue family already known. Exact manifestation noveltyUNKNOWN; physical noveltyNOT_CLAIMED; financial benefitconditional and unmeasured. Next gate: packaged-app reproduction and real affected decisions. No fleet saving estimate.
2. R-C2: residual part heat/solutal density/cavity geometry/drain-delay coupling. Narrow scheduling identity not matched in bounded search; transport effect not established. Existing rotation/targeted rinse is a strong comparator. Economic rank speculativeLOW until actual exchange and complete chemical/throughput costs are measured.
3. C-D2: allocate otherwise-wasted compressor-off coil cooling to a wetter batch, protect finishing product. Narrow allocation identityUNRESOLVED; rewet-control familyKNOWN; current ideal bound only0.235kWh/transition before costs and restoration. Low materiality without substantially different real hardware/duty.
PARKED AFTER ADVERSE PRIOR ART: C-D1 separate return treatment; R-C1 phase-compatible rinse/off-part recovery; M-01 carbonate-aware shutdown guard; M-02 gas-scour/CO2 chemistry separation. No positive incremental value over strongest available practice was demonstrated.
Ranking is by evidence/actionability and remaining material uncertainty. It is not a calibrated probability of discovery, expected commercial value or guarantee of large-scale savings.

B. SHARED GARDEN MAPPING CONTRACT
Each graph has physical THING/control-volume SPACE, timed STATE, EVENT and
PROCESS records; operator/controller ACTION and AGENCY are separate from
physical changes. Molecules, films, membranes and water have no agency,
ownership or delegation. RULE records bind conservation, constitutive models
or imposed constraints; CONTEXT binds operating scope; VALUE binds matched-
service economics; CLAIM records retain separate proof/experiment/novelty/
economic axes. CONSTRUCT, CONTRACT, STATE, RELATION, PROCESS, RULE and
PROJECTION are used as overlapping forms, not invented extra core objects.
Meaningful research relations include partOf, dependsOn, contextualizes,
derivedFrom, references, supports, contradicts, governs and qualified causes;
controls concerns authorized actuators. Unused relations are not multiplied
to inflate coverage. The edge lists below are physical/dependency projections,
not claims that every arrow is an admitted GSL cause relation.
All ten facets remain inspection obligations: batch/equipment identity;
physical scope; source/evidence status; operator authority; material effects
and safety; dependency invalidation; water/energy/time and solve termination;
industrial-data retention; audit explanation; fallback/recovery. Numeric
quality, pressure, sanitation and release limits remain UNKNOWN where no real
plant specification exists. Unknowns cannot be turned into release permissions.

Expansion is justified by distinct water, sensible/latent heat and actuation
paths, then by compressor-off energy/film storage. No plant was actuated.

D-I register: all are existing laws/requirements, not new fundamental laws.
I1 dry-air continuity; exact within leak-accounted control volume.

Audited motifs: (D07,D08)>D09>D10>D11 destroys separate treatment options at
mixing; D10/D13>D14/D15 checks heat accounting; D11>D12>D17 checks whether
condensate represents actual completed drying. Projection-Loss here concerns
lost actuation addressability as well as a reduced observable. Necessity-
Challenge asks whether all return air must be mixed before dehumidification.
Decision-Sufficiency compares additional branch measurements/routing cost with
changed admissible actions and actual whole-site value.
C-D1 solution candidate: maintain separate returns through evaporator allocation,
bypass the already-dry branch, then mix/reheat. Strong comparison includes an
optimized mixed-return bypass/coil-temperature arrangement AND already available
independent-return treatment designs. The latter are essential for novelty.

Equation contract: h(T,w) above, T Celsius; w kgwater/kgdryair; atmospheric
pressure101.325kPa. w_sat(T)=.62198 p_sat(T)/(101.325-p_sat(T)). Baseline mixed
return has w_m and h_m; targetw_s. With coilw_c=w_sat(T_c), processed fraction
alpha=(w_m-w_s)/(w_m-w_c). For totaldryairflowm:
 Qe=m alpha[h_m-h(T_c,w_c)-(w_m-w_c)*4.186T_c].
Candidate wet-only coil Qe=m_A[h_A-h(T_d,w_s)-(w_A-w_s)*4.186T_d].
Screening COPe=.45(T_c+273.15)/(65-T_c), not a measured machine map.
Wcomp=Qe/COPe; Qreject=Qe+Wcomp-Qreheat must be nonnegative, or explicit
auxiliary heat/work must be added. Units: Q in kW for massflowkg/s.

The first frozen synthetic state (wetreturn40C,w.025;supply60C,w.008) needed
unidentified extra product/heater heat; it was NOT silently accepted as an
adiabatic process. Post-freeze repair uses wetreturn40C,w.016 and drierreturn
55C,w.008; supply60C,w.008; each1kgdryair/s. Script industrial_physical_screens.py (drying replay) solves the
mixed-return coil/bypass optimum including endpoints. Conditional result:
34.843427kW baseline versus21.321233kW selective; both remove28.8kgwater/hour;
external heat rejection29.791034versus16.044085kW; product-boundary remaining
heat1.15392kW wetbranch and5.1044kW drierbranch. These are idealized calculations,
not verified hardware, measured savings or whole-cycle forecasts. Complete
product dynamics, coil contact, refrigerant map, fans and actual annual duty
are unresolved. An existing selective topology may achieve the same result.

C2 refinement: D10–D12/D13 transition expands to nine local T rows:
T01 running compressor, cold coil and liquid film;
T02 selected batch reaches endpoint;
T03 compressor stops, refrigerant relaxes;
T04 cooling reserve and retained liquid persist;
T05 return routing changes;
T06 coil continues condensation below exposed-stream dewpoint;
T07 dewpoint crossing permits film re-evaporation;
T08 drainage/isolation/next-batch decision;
T09 restart with matched energy/water boundary accounting.
For an explicit graph, this consolidation projects the source's conditional
prose into T01>T02,T02>T03,T03>T04,T04>T05,T05>T06,T06>T07,T06>T08,
T07>T08,T08>T09,T09>T01. These10edges are an integration-authored projection,
NOT a separately frozen or SAL-verified source edge list. Early isolation
T06>T08 avoids the optional re-evaporation branch.

Five added obligations: film-water balance; coil/refrigerant energy with all
ports; preserve finishing-batch humidity/quality; debit recreated cooling
reserve on restart; include ordinary isolation/drain-wait as an action.
Audited motif T04>T05>T06/T07>T08 concerns energy reserve versus watercarryback;
T08>T09>T01 prevents counting the same stored cooling twice.
C-D2 proposes diverting unavoidable compressor-off cold-coil reserve to another
wetter batch while protecting finishing product. For lumpedcoil Cc[kJ/K],
perfectcontact and NO residual refrigerant/ambient heat/fan work:
 Cc dT/dt=m_da[h(T_r,w_r)-h(T,w_sat(T))-(w_r-w_sat(T))*c_l T].
Integrate only to dewpoint; subtract film-inventory increase from collection.
Cc100kJ/K,T0=8C,wetreturn40C,w.020 givesidealcondensation.276847kg and totalcold
.470471kWh. If COP>=2 and reserve would otherwise be wasted, displaced electricity
is at most.235235kWh/transition before fan/valve/measurement costs. Equal-final-
state restoration can cancel it. This small conditional bound does not support
large economic benefit. No confirmed new design advantage survived.

Drying falsifiers/limits: existing topology already performs the same routing;
actual maps/fans eliminate gain; product quality/time changes; no compatible
wetbatch at transition; isolation preserves reserve better; residual refrigerant
heat invalidates bound; film carryover rewets product; restart cost cancels gain.

Five explicit audited motifs: R06>R07>R09 dissolved-to-solid handoff;

R-C1 solution candidate: chemistry-compatible displacement rinse reduces
retained dissolved aluminum before a pH-changing rinse; precipitation/recovery
moves off-part. Contract for fixed mixedbulk and calibrated k[s^-1]:
 c_f(t)=c_b+(c_0-c_b)exp(-kt),
 t>=ln[(c_0-c_b)/(c_star-c_b)]/k if c_0>c_star>c_b.
If c_b>=c_star, finite time does not establish release. c_star must be qualified
for actual subsequent pH/temperature/attachment, not equated with a handbook
solubility. Synthetic c0=100,cb=1,cstar=5mol/m3,k=.5/s gives6.41765s, only
arithmetic. No calibrated performance or saving. Precipitation regeneration,
phase-aware cleanup and countercurrent rinsing already have prior art.

Operators: dissolved/conductivity projection can omit attachedsolid; challenge
whether desmutting is unnecessary fails generally because alloy residues can
remain; measurements are useful only if they change a qualified decision at
positive net value. Falsifiers: unchanged residue across predicted timing;
strong existing recipe already performs condition; other alloy residues remain;
quality loss or extra bathload outweighs reduced water. Narrow recipe novelty
unresolved; broad novelty rejected, monetary benefit UNKNOWN.

Six added obligations: conserve all element phases; preserve wall energy across
queue; use composition AND temperature for density; preserve gravity/geometry
through poses; compare full resources instead of drag-out alone; retain thermal-
shock/corrosion/precipitation/quality/cycle constraints. Audited composition is
G02/G03>G04>G05>G06>G07: drain time reduces external film but may remove heat
needed for cavity exchange. Internal motion alone is not solute export.

R-C2 proposed geometry-qualified upper bound on hot-part pre-rinse dwell,
relocating remaining drain time after initial exchange. Screening contract:
 rho(c,T)=rho_ref[1-beta_T(T-Tref)]+Delta_rho_s(c).
Possible density inversion requires T_liquid-Tr>Delta_rho_s/(rho_ref beta_T).
If Tw(td)=Ta+(T0-Ta)exp(-td/tau_air) and liquid tracks wall, a screening limit is
 td<tau_air ln[(T0-Ta)/(Tr-Ta+Delta_rho_s/(rho_ref beta_T)+margin)].
This is NOT sufficient for blind-cavity renewal, nor a validated operating rule.
All temperature differences consistent; log dimensionless; tau andtd seconds.
Synthetic rho1000kg/m3,beta2.1e-4/K,densityexcess5kg/m3,T0=70C,Ta=Tr=20C,
tau120s gives~89s. Available50K offsets only10.5kg/m3 in that linear model;
original concentrated etching liquor may exceed this. Actual properties are
composition/temperature dependent. Positive local buoyancy can create closed
eddies with no meaningful cavity-mouth exchange. No transport simulation or
calibrated rinse-performance prediction was completed.
Comparators include existing rotation/air-assisted cavity cleaning, best drain/
rinse scheduling and a full heat/species/flow model. Known physics does not
alone negate the narrow scheduling idea; no exact disclosure found in bounded
search is also NOT novelty proof. Falsify if actual fluid has no applicable
thermal/density window, cavity exchange does not improve, chemical recovery
loss dominates, or an established practical method is superior at matched cost.
R-C2 status: narrow novelty UNKNOWN; broad convection physics known; screening
math only; economics UNKNOWN; low priority without actual parts/property data.

M-01 candidate: qualify flush/vent sequence against carbonate saturation across
future gas exchange rather than a conductivity-only endpoint.
Contract, bicarbonatedominant dilute model: Omega~C*Ca*Alk^2/pCO2; common
calcium/alkalinity dilutionfactorf gives Omega1/Omega0~f^3*p0/p1.
Synthetic Omega0=.8,f=.5,p0=.02atm,p1=.0004atm yields approximateOmega1=5;

supersaturation, NOT depositmass or membrane loss. ApproxOmega<=1 requires
f<=.29240177; CSTRwashQ*t/V>=1.229626485. Plugflow and real serialelements differ.
Conductivity equal-projection witness was NOT exactly constructed; changed
speciation can change conductivity. It remains an explicit unresolved test.
Falsifiers: absent/slow gascontact; activity corrections overturn prediction;

M-02 candidate: control gasflow for removal and gasCO2fraction to match local
liquid CO2 chemical potential. Localcontract N=kLa*V*(Cco2-KH*y*P), mol/s for
kLa/s,V m3,Cmol/m3,KHmol/(m3Pa),P Pa. y*=Cco2/(KH P),0<=y<=1. Frozen
Cco2=.00068mol/L,KH=.034mol/(L atm),P1atm gives2%CO2. This is a known
Henry-law setpoint, not itself a new cleaning-performance result. Equal gasflow
does not establish equal bubbleshear or10% cleaningnoninferiority.
Post-freeze attack: at fixedalkalinity,pH andequilibrium, freeCO2 and totalcarbon
are fixed. ExistingCO2/pH control can implement the same chemical objective.
With reactions/alkalinity changes, neither simple matching nor that equivalence
alone controls localscale. Aggregate matching ybar=sum(kVC)/sum(kVKH*P) can
hide local stripping andprecipitation; preserving an alreadybad state is notsafe.
Prior art has membrane gasscour plus separateCO2/pH control and RO studies
separating chemical versus bubblecleaning. Exact zero-fluxROimplementation
unresolved; broad dual-control relationship known. No money-saving survivor.
Falsifiers: equal/better optimized pH-scour control; chemistry/shear gradients
violate model; membrane gasuse disallowed; cost of gas/blending/sensors exceeds
benefit; zero-fluxstate preserves scale or impairs dissolution. Realwater,
membrane compatibility, dutycycle, removal metrics and all cost terms unknown.

PRESSURE REFERENCE CLOSURE — candidate freeze

At unchanged p1=100, existing code predicts $9214.95/y savings instead of zero. At p1=90, existing predicts $13307.12/y; normalized measured-baseline model predicts $4624.90/y.
Equation repair: F(p,a)=((p+a)/a)^(.395/1.395)-1; P1=P0*F(p1,a)/F(p0,a). Preserve explicit power basis and reference state.
Scope: fixed flow, efficiency, inlet conditions, same empirical pressure-law approximation. No prediction for unloading, leak-flow change, control switching, demand, transients.

4.12.1 OUTCOME AND WHAT THE NUMBERS MEAN

The request was for ten more novel ideas with savings. We investigated ten
additional industrial interventions rather than declaring ten discoveries.
The requested scientific novelty criterion has NOT been met in this batch.
Seven cases have positive arithmetic under explicit hypothetical plant inputs;
none establishes a novel increment over the strongest available engineering.
One proposal loses to ordinary control, one fails the declared economic screen,
and one has no demonstrated diagnostic advantage and only a cost threshold.
These failures neither establish domain saturation nor invalidate the method.

All financial amounts below are USD PER FACILITY PER YEAR after the stated
recurring costs and annualized incremental capital. They are NOT global savings,
measured savings, current vendor quotations, or probability-weighted forecasts.
The assumed 8% discount rate and 10-year life give CRF=0.1490294887/year.
Seven positive figures are opportunities conditional on the specified physical
gain and comparison, not seven qualified investments. No success probability is
estimated. The rows must not be summed into a Garden or worldwide benefit.

Case | Process/interface | Conditional annual net | Controlling qualification
H1 | Aluminium decoating -> ash removal -> compaction -> all recovery paths
   | +$650,970.51 | Requires UNMEASURED extra 5 kg saleable Al/t feed at 100 kt/y;
     existing hot ash separation is prior art; all fines/dross recovery counted.
H2 | Steel surface/corner phase reset -> core self-reheat -> hot charging
   | +$3,438,185.89 | IDEAL retained-heat scenario at 1 Mt/y versus full cooling;
     core sequence already disclosed; no increment over modern selective cooling.
H3 | Cement hot mineral separation -> chloride condensation -> return/purge
   | +$237,637.18 | Requires UNMEASURED additional clean return of 14 kg/t at
     1 Mt/y; modern coarse-return and heat-recovery baseline may erase benefit.
U1 | Contaminated condensate -> flash split -> separate qualified destinations
   | +$83,811.15 | Assumed eligible duty 10%, 30 t/h; chemistry and leak-repair
     comparator unresolved; established stripping/recovery mechanism.
U2 | Tower salt inventory -> combined blowdown -> receiving treatment
   | REJECTED | Ordinary continuous blowdown has lower modeled receiver peak
     at identical periodic water/salt totals; apparent +$45,670 is not credited.
U3 | Stratified storage -> cold and tolerant loads -> full recharge
   | +$50,196.37 | Assumed existing 4,000 m3 usable layer, 200 matched cycles/y;
     equivalent modern multi-temperature routing ties the proposed action.
F1 | Sectional defrost -> vapor collector -> drainage -> restored cooling
   | NEGATIVE | Cooling-only net -$28,205.90; generous separate electric-heat
     replacement sensitivity -$18,305.90. Original hot-gas case cannot assume
     electric-resistance credit. Neither result proves universal unprofitability.
F2 | Hot plate integrity -> pressure-coded tracer -> independent startup gate
   | UNKNOWN | Needs >$18,461.18/y genuinely additional avoided loss. A strong
     hot constant-pressure tracer test is more sensitive in the modeled noise.
M1 | Thin-wall machining -> two safe nonzero preloads -> released acceptance
   | +$91,763.72 | Assumes UNMEASURED 1 percentage-point scrap improvement on
     100,000 parts/y; compensation family known, narrow integration unresolved.
M2 | Resin identity in separate hot-runner branches -> endpoint purge -> restart
   | +$62,990.11 | 500 assumed changes/y versus all-open purge; direct published
     selective-gating prior art ties the material result; no time gain credited.

Scale examples are deliberately explicit. H2's million-dollar figure belongs to
one hypothetical million-tonne steel operation, not all steel plants. H1's figure
belongs to one hypothetical 100,000-tonne scrap operation. Finding a real site
with these unexploited conditions remains outstanding. A large affected industry
is not evidence of an eligible population, incremental recovery, or adoption.

The 118 IDs and 166 edges are explicit finite graph records, not a fraction of
all real industrial situations. Graphs include temporal/material flow, control,
dependency and next-cycle feedback edges; they are not all chronological time
steps. Constraint families overlap across cases, so 92 is a count of scoped
records, not 92 independent laws. Heat applicability is stated by node/stream
scope and is not inflated into a synthetic binding total. The Python replay
contains all ten adjacency graphs and checks references and duplicate edges.
Text below binds situations to the applicable invariant and equation contracts.

Ten original intervention claims were checked; one H1 repair did not become an
eleventh idea. Three operators were applied to each case:
- Projection-Loss constructs distinguishable future outcomes/actions from an
  equal coarse observation, then asks whether strong engineering already retains
  the distinction. Observation nonclosure alone does not imply novelty.
- Necessity-Challenge tests an expensive prerequisite while preserving actual
  physical, safety, quality and terminal-state obligations.
- Decision-Sufficiency compares the best admissible action with extra information
  to the strongest practical existing action, less measurement/implementation
  costs. The value-of-information mathematics is established, not a new theorem.

Known mechanisms and unresolved interfaces are individually named in each
packet. No worldwide unknown-count or arbitrary coverage percentage is given.
Examples of still-unresolved relationships: metallic versus elemental Al yield
in classifier fines; feed-specific chloride phase partition; safe fixture-contact
response versus released dimensions; actual local plate stress and interfering
tracer signals; residual defrost vapor beyond a strong hood. These are research
questions, not supported new effects. Physical experiments and measured economic
results in this batch: ZERO.

4.12.4 QUANTITATIVE RESULTS THAT SURVIVED ARITHMETIC CHECKS

1. U2 conservation closes, but ordinary continuous control defeats the proposed
   staggering. Receiver peaks: synchronous 3033.421 mg/L; staggered 1599.908;
   continuous 1043.478, at the same 216 m3 and 1296 kg salt per 12 hours. No
   biological injury claim is inferred from salinity alone.
2. M1's linear model predicts 10 micrometres free shape with 1.118 micrometres
   probe-noise standard deviation. Allowed quadratic curvature creates a
   5-micrometre bias, exposing its limited applicability; no scrap gain measured.
3. M2's repaired safe-flow schedule uses 13.81551 kg versus 69.07755 kg all-open
   purge in a two-compartment model. Both take 69.07755 minutes. Known selective
   gating ties the result. Final old-species inventories differ by 0.001 kg,
   explicitly recorded; actual restart qualification may consume more resin.
4. F2's solute modulation predicts 0.19164 uS/cm amplitude. A compliance-only
   intact plate defeats pressure/volume diagnosis, motivating the tracer repair.
   A calibrated hot DC tracer comparator gives 5 uS/cm in the same example and
   stronger white-noise sensitivity. Synthetic null performance is not a field
   false-positive estimate.
5. H2's closed two-lump model reaches 750 C surface temperature in 44.8329 s,
   with 939.4737 C core temperature and conserved mean 930 C. It predicts only
   heat redistribution, not phase feasibility or crack-free industrial output.
6. H1R's plausible-looking +$185,485 repair scenario becomes -$14,515 when even
   50% existing fines recovery is included. Comparator choice changes the sign.

The replay exercises 17 non-tautological heat/material checks, 47 utility checks,
56 manufacturing checks and the food equation/null/100-seed synthetic comparison.
The original heat role reported 18; integration removed one tautological zero
multiplication check. Historical packet/reviewer counts are retained below with
this correction. Check counts are software/model receipts, not scientific sample
sizes. The graph adjacency checks are separate from physical validation.

A quantitative falsifiable contract exists in every case. A newly written
contract is not necessarily a new prediction: established VLE, heat transfer,
compartment flow and fixture mechanics derive these outcomes too. Consequently,
mathematical consistency and executable simulation do not upgrade novelty status.

4.12.5 RANKED DISPOSITION AND ECONOMIC GATES

No case survives as a proven novel positive-value improvement. Ranking below is
for the next discriminating research question, not investment advice or a claim
of high success probability. No additional physical test is implied to be running.

Priority 1 — M1: narrow two-nonzero-preload fixture protocol. Threshold: >0.2353
percentage points additional scrap reduction under the stated cost assumptions.
First test is released-geometry error versus modern adaptive compensation, with
contact stability and nonlinear thermomechanics. Near prior art makes success
uncertain; do not fund from the assumed +$91.8k value alone.

Priority 2 — H1/H1R: net metallic Al in the ash/fines interface. Large throughput
makes it material, but the core interventions are old. Require genuine net yield
beyond optimized decoating, separation AND dross/fines recovery. Original H1
break-even 1.745 kg/t; H1R additional recovery break-even 0.6726 kg/t at its own
cost. The repair already fails an illustrative 50%-recovery comparator.

Priority 3 — F2: only if measured colored interference defeats a fully calibrated
hot constant-pressure tracer baseline. Current white-noise comparison is adverse.
Requires independently supported added avoided loss >$18,461/y; no current value
estimate or superior detection has been established.

Known-practice audits — H2, H3, U1, U3 and M2 can identify adoption opportunities
at specific facilities. Their conditional per-facility figures justify asking
for plant evidence, not claiming new science. Stop novelty expansion of the
already matched H2/M2 core sequences. U1 must include fixing the source leak as a
comparator; long contamination duty cannot simply be assumed to persist.

Park under present contracts — U2 fails the stronger comparator; F1's declared
scenarios fail costs. Changing constraints after seeing failure is a new
hypothesis and must receive a new freeze, not retroactively rescue these claims.

For every live research branch the economic acceptance condition is:
  Delta_net = measured additional avoidable annual cost versus best baseline
              - recurring incremental costs - CRF * installed incremental capex
              - any separately uncounted quality, downtime or validation costs.
No adoption/worldwide model is provided because eligible installed populations,
measured improvements, implementation costs and baseline overlap are UNKNOWN.
